## 3. Country-by-Country Route Audits (All 28 Origins)

### Origin: AT — E-Control (AIB) / AGCS Biomethan Register (ERGaR)
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `CONNECTED` | ERGaR: `PARTICIPANT` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Austrian gas transmission grid is interconnected with DE, CZ, SK, HU, SI, IT within the single EU mass-balance system.)
- **Supported Volume Rule**: Subsidised production GOs cannot be exported via AIB; gas support is investment grants; SEG confirms exported gas can use ERGaR RED MB scheme [`SUPPORTED_MUST_STAY`] - condition applied to PoS routes: unsupported volumes only: E-Control excludes GOs from subsidised plants from international transfer and trade; gas support is mostly investment grants
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — E-Control DP C.3: Cancelation for usage in another Domain (i.e. Ex Domain Cancellations) is not allowed (in exceptional cases only within AIB Members and under precondition of cancellation agreement).

- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `ELECTRICITY_ONLY` | ERGaR: `NONE` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Belgian Fluxys grid is interconnected with NL, DE, FR, LU, GB and connects Zeebrugge LNG terminal.)
- **PoS Export Status**: `OPEN` (Q-ORIG-BE) - Biomethane in Belgium is produced in Flanders (small volumes, no export rule found, lean yes) and Wallonia (national non-EECS GO tied to Walloon CHP green certificates, PoS export rule not found); Brussels has no production. No public source settles whether PoS may be exported by mass balance.
- **Supported Volume Rule**: Walloon biomethane GO ties green value to local CHP green certificates; Flanders has small volumes with no export ban documented [`OPEN`] - condition applied to PoS routes: Walloon biomethane GOs are tied to local CHP green certificates; Flemish volumes are small with no documented export ban
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `CONDITIONAL` — Wallonia DP C.3.5 / Flanders DP: ex-domain requires agreement with destination issuing body (conditional under SYNTHESIS-NOTES.md).

### Origin: DK — Energinet
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `APPLICANT` | ERGaR: `PARTICIPANT` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Danish system is interconnected with Germany (Ellund) and Sweden (Dragør).)
- **Supported Volume Rule**: 2024+ tender volumes get no GOs; old pristillæg scheme volumes export GOs and PoS widely (Commission confirmed subsidised volumes eligible under FuelEU) [`OPEN`] - condition applied to PoS routes: 2024+ tender volumes carry no GOs and PoS use abroad depends on the destination accepting supported volumes; old pristillaeg and unsupported volumes are exported widely
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `ALLOWED` — Energinet cross-border page: Ex-domain cancellation is still allowed for countries without a registry or if the registry is not a member of the ERGaR hub.

### Origin: FR — EEX (Powernext)
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `CONNECTED` | ERGaR: `NONE` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (France is centrally interconnected with BE, DE, CH, ES, LU, IT.)
- **Supported Volume Rule**: GOs of plants under obligation d'achat or complément de rémunération belong to the French State (DGEC) and are auctioned; producer can exit only by reimbursing subsidies [`STATE_OWNS_ATTRIBUTES`] - condition applied to PoS routes: only volumes outside the obligation d'achat / complement de remuneration: the State owns those GOs and auctions them, and PoS export of supported gas is not settled by a public source
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `CONDITIONAL` — DP Part III E.10: Ex-domain permitted only for European countries without AIB Hub access, subject to bilateral EDC agreement.

### Origin: GB — GGCS (Green Gas Certification Scheme / REAL)
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `NONE` | ERGaR: `PARTICIPANT` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `NO` (United Kingdom is a third country; European Commission states third-country grids are outside the single mass-balance system for EU compliance.)
- **Supported Volume Rule**: GGSS pays quarterly tariff for GB grid injection; cannot double-claim RTFO [`SUPPORTED_MUST_STAY`] - condition applied to PoS routes: unsupported volumes only: no Green Gas Support Scheme payment where an RTFC was issued for the same biomethane
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — GGCS Guidance Doc 7: Transfers to/from registries other than ERGaR CoO participants are not possible.

### Origin: GR — DAPEEP

- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Interconnected with Bulgaria via IGB interconnector and Kulata/Sidirokastro.)
- **PoS Export Status**: `OPEN` (Q-ORIG-GR) - Research found no public source on a biomethane support scheme or export rule in Greece; export of PoS by mass balance from Greece is open (the grid is inside the EU single mass-balance system, volumes are believed negligible but not verified).
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — Law 5215/2025 created framework, but DAPEEP is electricity-only at AIB; no gas registry module operating.

### Origin: IE — Gas Networks Ireland (GNI)
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `OBSERVER` | ERGaR: `NONE` | Operational: `false`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `NO` (Ireland is physically connected to the gas grid solely via Scotland (GB Moffat interconnector, one-way flow); European Commission detailed opinion on Irish RHO states pipeline biomethane from UK or continent cannot count towards Irish RED targets.)
- **PoS Export Status**: `OPEN` (Q-EU-1) - Irish gas reaches the continent only through Great Britain. The German THG statute (BImSchG s.37b(6) n.F.) literally covers biomethane injected anywhere in the EU excise territory, which includes Ireland, but the Commission reading (detailed opinion on the Irish Renewable Heat Obligation, quoted by Eurogas, secondary) does not treat Ireland as part of the single EU mass-balance system because its only gas link is via Great Britain, and the Moffat interconnector is configured one-way GB to IE (secondary). The two readings conflict, so Irish-origin PoS is OPEN for every destination that would otherwise accept foreign gas.
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `UNKNOWN` — Manual Excel pilot registry; cannot connect to AIB or ERGaR; replacement registry planned Q1 2027.

GOs for gas produced before Amber Grid joined the AIB Gas Scheme cannot be exported (DP E.1.15). | N | FR_TIRUERT | Government amendment 3492 deleted bioGNV from TIRUERT 2026; scheme is restricted to petrol and diesel streams. *Conditions:* None for 2026. From 1 Jan 2027 the draft IRICC mechanism replaces TIRUERT and may count biogas via GOs held by the distributor plus a sustainability proof; OPEN (draft, not law, silent on imported injection). |

| IE | N | - | RULE | Destination registry IE (Gas Networks Ireland (GNI)) is not operational or not connected to any international gas GO hub (AIB/ERGaR). | O | IE_RTFO | GNI operates pilot recognition for foreign mass-balance certificates, but no standardised procedure exists. *Conditions:* GNI Proof of Origin pilot recognition; delivery via Moffat interconnector; NORA acceptance. Art. 12 permits foreign plants only under a Dir 2009/28 cooperation agreement with reciprocity (none in force). *Conditions:* Italian gas network injection required. |

| LV | P (RULE) | AIB | RULE | Both registries are on the AIB gas hub and no domain protocol bars the lane, but only test-size transfers have been observed (5 MWh or less; not a commercial-size flow), so the lane rests on hub rules. Conditions: legacy pre-membership Lithuanian GOs cannot be exported. **Condition:** GOs for gas produced before Amber Grid joined the AIB Gas Scheme cannot be exported (DP E.1.15). | O | LV_TRANSPORT | The GO corridor LT>LV exists, but whether an AIB-imported GO can be redeemed for the LV national scheme (end use, evidence and PoS) is not verified end to end; counting is unconfirmed until the registry or authority answers. *Conditions:* Biomethane sold through grid requires a gas GO issued/registered under Latvian energy law (Conexus) plus mass-balance proof. |

| SE | P (RULE) | AIB | RULE | Transfer via AIB Hub possible, but Cesar accepts only EU or treaty-state EECS GOs produced within the last 12 months; and legacy pre-membership Lithuanian GOs cannot be exported; and no Swedish gas transfer has been recorded yet and the Swedish DP is still in the future tense. **Condition:** Production within the last 12 months; EU or treaty-state EECS GOs only (Swedish DP E.10.2, E.13.3). GOs for gas produced before Amber Grid joined the AIB Gas Scheme cannot be exported (DP E.1.15). No Swedish gas GO transfer is recorded on the AIB hub yet (EECS gas from 1 Sep 2026) and the Swedish DP Release 5 (26 Aug 2026) is still in the future tense; unproven in practice. | P | SE_TAX | Swedish law has no domestic-only restriction; Skatteverket and Energimyndigheten recognise imported biogas via western Swedish gas network (1,874 GWh net imported 2023). *Conditions:* Hållbarhetsbesked and anläggningsbesked under Lag 2010:598; physical delivery through western Swedish gas grid (Dragør link). |
| SI | N | - | RULE | Destination registry SI (Agencija za energijo / Borzen) is not operational or not connected to any international gas GO hub (AIB/ERGaR). | O | SI_TRANSPORT | Statute is origin-neutral, but administrative evidence rules for cross-border grid biomethane are unsettled. *Conditions:* Origin-neutral 2% biogas quota for gaseous fuels; mass balance evidence rules open. |
| SK | P (PUBLISHED) | ERGAR | PUBLISHED | ERGaR April 2026 announcement confirms bilateral exchange between Lithuania and Slovakia. | P | SK_TRANSPORT | Eligible via imported GO into SK registry combined with mass-balance PoS. *Conditions:* EECS GO imported via AIB or ERGaR into SPP-d; plant grid-connected; foreign PoS recognized (§14b(7)); GO cancelled for quota. |

### Origin: LU — ILR (Institut Luxembourgeois de Régulation)
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `ELECTRICITY_ONLY` | ERGaR: `NONE` | Operational: `false`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Integrated into BeLux single gas balancing market with Belgium; connected to BE, DE, FR.)
- **PoS Export Status**: `OPEN` (Q-ORIG-LU) - Research found no public source on a biomethane support scheme or export rule in Luxembourg; export of PoS by mass balance from Luxembourg is open (the grid is inside the EU single mass-balance system, volumes are believed negligible but not verified). GOs for remunerated biogas also remain State property.
- **Supported Volume Rule**: RGD of 4 Nov 2022 Art. 11ter: GOs for remunerated biogas remain the property of the State [`STATE_OWNS_ATTRIBUTES`] - condition applied to PoS routes: GOs of remunerated biogas remain State property (RGD 4 Nov 2022 Art. 11ter)
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — RGD of 4 Nov 2022 establishes framework, but ILR is electricity-only on AIB; no operating gas module.

- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `CONNECTED` | ERGaR: `LEFT` | Operational: `true`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Core European gas hub interconnected with Germany, Belgium, and via BBL pipeline with the UK.)

- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — DP C.3.4: Cancellations for usage in another Domain (i.e., Ex Domain Cancellations) are not allowed.

### Origin: RO — ANRE
- **Hub Connectivity**: AIB: `OBSERVER` | ERGaR: `NONE` | Operational: `false`
- **EU Mass-Balance System**: `YES` (Transgaz network interconnected with Hungary, Bulgaria, Ukraine, Moldova.)
- **PoS Export Status**: `OPEN` (Q-ORIG-RO) - Research found no public source on a biomethane support scheme or export rule in Romania; export of PoS by mass balance from Romania is open (the grid is inside the EU single mass-balance system, volumes are believed negligible but not verified). No biomethane production facility existed per the energy strategy.
- **Ex-Domain Cancellation Policy**: `NOT_ALLOWED` — ANRE is an AIB observer only; gas GO regulations mandated for end of 2026 with market opening in 2027.
